32   protected[quality] def literalsNeeded(pos: Int, typ: String = "String"): Nothing =
33     if (pos == -1)
34       qualityException("Cannot setup Quality Expression with non-literals")
35     else
36       qualityException(s"Quality Expression requires a $typ literal in (starts with position 0) position $pos")
37 
38   protected[quality] def literalsNeeded: Nothing = literalsNeeded( -1 )
39 
40   protected[quality] def getLong(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) =
41     exp match {
42       case Literal(seed: Long, LongType) => seed
43       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos, "Long")
44     }
45   protected[quality] def getInteger(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) =
46     exp match {
47       case Literal(seed: Int, IntegerType) => seed
48       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos, "Integer")
49     }
50   protected[quality] def getBoolean(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) =
51     exp match {
52       case Literal(seed: Boolean, BooleanType) => seed
53       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos, "Boolean")
54     }
55   protected[quality] def getString(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) =
56     exp match {
57       case Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType) => str.toString()
58       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos)
59     }
60 
61   protected[quality] def getBinary(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1): Array[Byte] =
62     exp match {
63       case Literal(ar: Array[Byte], _: BinaryType) => ar
64       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos, "Binary")
65     }

67   protected[quality] val mustKeepNames = Set(LambdaFunctions.PlaceHolder,
68     LambdaFunctions.Lambda, LambdaFunctions.CallFun)

86   private val noopAdd = (dt: DataType) => None
87 
88   /**
89    * Provides the default monoidal add for a dataType, used for merging summed results when aggregating
90    *
91    * @param dataType
92    * @return
93    */
94   def defaultAdd(dataType: DataType, extension: DataType => Option[(Expression, Expression) => Expression] = noopAdd): Option[(Expression, Expression) => Expression] =
95     dataType match {
96       case _: MapType => Some((left, right) => MapMerge(Seq(left, right), (dataType: DataType) => defaultAdd(dataType, extension)))
97       case _: IntegerType | LongType | DoubleType =>
98         Some((left, right) => add(left, right, null))
99       case a: DecimalType =>
100         Some((left, right) => add(left, right, a))
101       case _ => extension(dataType)
102     }
103 
104   /**
105    * Provides the default monoidal Zero for a dataType, used for defaults when aggregating
106    *
107    * @param dataType
108    * @return
109    */
110   def defaultZero(dataType: DataType): Option[Any] =
111     dataType match {
112       case _: MapType => Some(EmptyMap)
113       case _: IntegerType | LongType => Some(0L)
114       case _: DoubleType => Some(0.0)
115       case d: DecimalType => Some(Decimal.createUnsafe(0, d.precision, d.scale))
116       case _ => None
117     }
118 
119   /**
120    * Wrap to provide the default lookup for registerFunctions to change type parsing from DDL based to other or
121    * when None to add additional lookups should ddl fail
122    *
123    * @param string
124    * @return
125    */
126   def defaultParseTypes(string: String): Option[DataType] =
127     try {
128       Some(
129         DataType.fromDDL(string)
130       )
131     } catch {
132       case _: Throwable => None
133     }
134 
135   // #12 - use underscore names but keep the old camel case approach around for compat
136   def registerWithChecks(registerFunction: (String, Seq[Expression] => Expression) => Unit, name: String, argsf: Seq[Expression] => Expression, paramNumbers: Set[Int] = Set.empty, minimum: Int = -1) = {
137     val create =
138       if (paramNumbers.isEmpty && minimum == -1 )
139         argsf
140       else
141         (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
142           if ((paramNumbers.nonEmpty && !paramNumbers.contains(exps.size)) || (minimum > exps.size)) {
143             val sizeerr =
144               if (paramNumbers.nonEmpty)
145                 s"Valid parameter counts are ${paramNumbers.mkString(", ")}"
146               else
147                 s"A minimum of $minimum parameters is required."
148             throw QualityException(s"Wrong number of arguments provided to Quality function $name, received ${exps.size}. $sizeerr")
149           }
150           argsf(exps)
151         }
152 
153     registerFunction(name, create)
154 
155     if (!mustKeepNames(name)) {
156       registerFunction(name.replaceAll("_",""), create)
157     }
158   }
159 
160   /**
161    * Must be called before using any functions like Passed, Failed or Probability(X)
162    * @param parseTypes override type parsing (e.g. DDL, defaults to defaultParseTypes / DataType.fromDDL)
163    * @param zero override zero creation for aggExpr (defaults to defaultZero)
164    * @param add override the "add" function for aggExpr types (defaults to defaultAdd(dataType))
165    * @param writer override the printCode and printExpr print writing function (defaults to println)
166    * @param registerFunction function to register the sql extensions
167    */
168   def registerQualityFunctions(parseTypes: String => Option[DataType] = defaultParseTypes,
169                                zero: DataType => Option[Any] = defaultZero,
170                                add: DataType => Option[(Expression, Expression) => Expression] = (dataType: DataType) => defaultAdd(dataType),
171                                mapCompare: DataType => Option[(Any, Any) => Int] = (dataType: DataType) => utils.defaultMapCompare(dataType),
172                                writer: String => Unit = println,
173                                registerFunction: (String, Seq[Expression] => Expression) => Unit =
174                                 (n, f) => ShimUtils.registerFunction(SparkSession.active)(n,f)
175                               ) : Unit = {
176 
177     def register(name: String, argsf: Seq[Expression] => Expression, paramNumbers: Set[Int] = Set.empty, minimum: Int = -1) =
178       registerWithChecks(registerFunction, name, argsf, paramNumbers, minimum)
179 
180     def parse(exp: Expression) = {
181       val Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType) = exp // only accept type as string
182       parseTypes(str.toString).getOrElse(qualityException(s"Could not parse the type $str"))
183     }
184 
185     def getMap(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) = exp match {
186       case l: Literal if l.dataType.isInstanceOf[MapType] =>
187         MapUtils.toScalaMap(l.value.asInstanceOf[ArrayBasedMapData], StringType, StringType).map(p => (p._1.toString, p._2.toString))
188       case c: CreateMap if c.children.grouped(2).forall{
189         case Seq(Literal(_: UTF8String, StringType), _: Literal) =>
190           true
191         case _ => false
192       } =>
193         c.children.grouped(2).map{
194           case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType), value: Literal) =>
195             str.toString -> value.value.toString()
196         }.toMap
197       case _ => throw QualityException(s"Could not process a literal map with expression $exp index $pos")
198     }

241     def strType(exp: Expression) = {

246     register(LambdaFunctions.PlaceHolder, {
247       case Seq(e, Literal(bol: Boolean, BooleanType)) =>
248         PlaceHolderExpression(parse(e), bol)
249       case Seq(e) =>
250         PlaceHolderExpression(parse(e))
251       case _ =>
252         PlaceHolderExpression(LongType)
253     }, Set(0, 1, 2))

255     /* Note - both Lambda and CallFun are only called in top level expressions,
256           nested calls are handled within the lambda expression
257           creation that "calls" this. */
258 
259     register(LambdaFunctions.Lambda, {
260       case Seq(fun: FunForward) =>
261         val res = FunCall(fun)
262         res
263       case Seq(fun: FunN) =>
264         // placeholders that are 1:1
265         val res = fun.function
266         res
267     }, Set(1))
268 
269     register(LambdaFunctions.CallFun, {
270       case (fun@ FunN(_, l@ SLambdaFunction(ff : FunForward, _, _), _, _, _, _)) +: args =>
271         processTopCallFun(fun, l, ff, args)
272       case t => qualityException(s"${LambdaFunctions.CallFun} should only be used to process partially applied functions returned by a user lambda, got $t instead")
273     }, minimum = 1)

275     val afx = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
276       val (sumType, filter, sum, count) =
277         exps.size match {
278           case 3 =>
279             // attempt to take a look at exps1 to identify if it's a FunN or mapWith
280             val typ =
281               exps(1) match {
282                 case FunN(Seq(RefExpression(dataType, _, _)), _, _, _, _, _) => dataType // would default to long anyway
283                 case MapTransform(RefExpression(t: MapType, _, _), _, _, _) => t
284                 case _ => LongType
285               }
286             (typ, exps(0), exps(1), exps(2))
287           case 4 =>
288             val Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType) = exps(0) // only accept type as string
289             if (str.toString == "NO_REWRITE")
290             // signal not to replace types
291             (null, exps(1), exps(2), exps(3))
292               else
293               (parse(exps(0)), exps(1), exps(2), exps(3))
294         }
295       AggregateExpressions(sumType, filter, sum, count, zero, add)
296     }
297     register("agg_Expr", afx, Set(3, 4))
298 
299     register("sum_With", (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
300       val (dataType, origExp) = exps.size match {
301         case 1 => (LongType, exps(0))
302         // backwards compat
303         case 2 => (parse(exps(0)), exps(1))
304       }
305       FunN(Seq(RefExpression(dataType)), origExp, Some("sum_With"), usedAsLambda = true)
306     }, Set(1, 2))
307 
308     val ff2 = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
309       // real type for param1 is changed by aggrExpr, but last works for all compat as well
310       val (sumType, exp) =
311         exps.size match {
312           case 1 => (LongType, exps(0))
313           case 2 => (parse(exps(0)), exps(1)) // support the NO_REWRITE override case
314         }
315 
316       FunN(Seq(RefExpression(sumType), RefExpression(LongType)), exp, Some("results_With"), usedAsLambda = true)
317     }
318     register("results_With", ff2, Set(1, 2))
319 
320     val mapFX = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => exps.size match {
321       case 3 =>
322         // parse it for old sql to support backwards aggExpr
323         MapTransform.create(RefExpression(parse(exps(0))), exps(1), exps(2), zero)
324       case 2 =>
325         // default to LongType, aggrExpr must fix, 2nd param is key, third the func manipulating the key
326         MapTransform.create(RefExpression(MapType(LongType, LongType)), exps(0), exps(1), zero)
327     }
328     register("map_With", mapFX, Set(2, 3))

330     def aggFWith(fun: String) = (what: String) => (exps: Seq[Expression]) => expression(
331       if (exps.size == 0)
332         functions.expr(s"$fun( $what )")
333       else
334         functions.expr(s"$fun('${strType(exps(0))}', $what )")
335       )
336 
337     val retWith = aggFWith("results_With")
338 
339     // common cases
340     register("meanF", retWith("(sum, count) -> sum / count"), Set(0, 1))

342     val sumWith = aggFWith("sum_With")

344     val incX = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => exps match {
345       case Seq(x: AttributeReference) =>
346         val name = x.qualifier.mkString(".") + x.name // that is bad code man should be option
347         sumWith(s"sum -> sum + $name")(Seq())
348       case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType)) =>
349         // case for type passing
350         sumWith("sum -> sum + 1")(exps)
351       case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType), x: AttributeReference) =>
352         val name = x.qualifier.mkString(".") + x.name
353         sumWith(s"sum -> sum + $name")(Seq(exps(0))) // keep the type, drop the attr
354       case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType), y) =>
355         qualityException(INC_REWRITE_GENEXP_ERR_MSG)
356       case Seq( y ) =>
357         val SLambdaFunction(a: Add, Seq(sum: UnresolvedNamedLambdaVariable), hidden ) = expression(functions.expr("sumWith(sum -> sum + 1)")).children(0)
358         import ShimUtils.{add => addf}
359         // could be a cast around x or three attributes plusing each other or....
360         FunN(Seq(RefExpression(LongType)),
361           SLambdaFunction(addf(a.left, y, LongType), Seq(sum), hidden )
362           , Some("inc"), usedAsLambda = true) // keep the type
363       case Seq() => expression(functions.expr(s"sumWith(sum -> sum + 1)"))
364     }
365     register("inc", incX, Set(1, 0, 2))
366 
367     // return sum
368     register("return_Sum", retWith("(sum, count) -> sum"), Set(0, 1))
369     def getRandom(exp: Expression, pos: Int) = {
370       val str = getString(exp, pos)
371       RandomSource.valueOf(str)
372     }
373 
374     // random generators
375     val brf = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
376       //numBytes: Int, randomSource: RandomSource, seed: Long constructor but needs to use random, seed, numbytes
377       val (numBytes: Int, randomSource, seed: Long) =
378         exps.size match {
379           case 0 => (16, RandomSource.XO_RO_SHI_RO_128_PP, 0L)
380           case 1 => (16, getRandom(exps(0), 0), 0L)
381           case 2 => (16, getRandom(exps(0), 0), getLong(exps(1), 1))
382           case 3 => (getInteger(exps(2), 2), getRandom(exps(0), 0), getLong(exps(1), 1))
383           case _ => literalsNeeded
384         }
385 
386       RandomBytes(numBytes, randomSource, seed)
387     }
388     register("rng_Bytes", brf, Set(0,1,2,3))
389 
390     // random generators
391     val lrf = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
392 
393       //randomSource: RandomSource, seed: Long constructor but needs to use random, seed, numbytes
394       val (randomSource, seed: Long) =
395         exps.size match {
396           case 0 => (RandomSource.XO_RO_SHI_RO_128_PP, 0L)
397           case 1 => (getRandom(exps(0), 0), 0L)
398           case 2 => (getRandom(exps(0), 0), getLong(exps(1), 1))
399           case _ => literalsNeeded
400         }
401 
402       RandomLongs.create(randomSource, seed)
403     }
404     register("rng", lrf, Set(0,1,2))

423     val longPairEqual = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
424       val Seq(Literal(a, StringType), Literal(b, StringType)) = exps
425 
426       def lower(a: Any) = UnresolvedAttribute(s"${a}_lower")
427 
428       def higher(a: Any) = UnresolvedAttribute(s"${a}_higher")
429 
430       And(EqualTo(lower(a), lower(b)), EqualTo(higher(a), higher(b)))
431     }
432     register("long_Pair_Equal", longPairEqual, Set(2))
433 
434     val idEqual = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
435       val Seq(Literal(a, StringType), Literal(b, StringType)) = exps
436       def attr(a: Any, field: String) = UnresolvedAttribute(s"${a}_$field")
437 
438       And(And(EqualTo(attr(a, "base"), attr(b, "base")),
439         EqualTo(attr(a, "i0"), attr(b, "i0"))),
440         EqualTo(attr(a, "i1"), attr(b, "i1")))
441     }
442     register("id_equal", idEqual, Set(2))

448     def fieldsToHash(asStruct: Boolean, factory: String => DigestFactory) = (exps: Seq[Expression]) =>
449       exps.size match {
450         case a if a > 3 =>
451           val digestImpl = getString(exps(0), 0)
452 
453           HashFunctionsExpression(exps.drop(1), digestImpl, asStruct, factory(digestImpl))
454 
455         case _ => literalsNeeded
456       }
457 
458     register("digest_To_Longs_Struct", fieldsToHash(true, MessageDigestFactory), minimum = 2)
459     register("digest_To_Longs",  fieldsToHash(false, MessageDigestFactory), minimum = 2)
460 
461     def fieldBasedID(factory: String => DigestFactory) = (exps: Seq[Expression]) =>
462       exps.size match {
463         case a if a > 3 =>
464           val digestImpl = getString(exps(1), 1)
465           val prefix = getString(exps.head, 0)
466 
467           GenericLongBasedIDExpression(model.FieldBasedID,
468             HashFunctionsExpression(exps.drop(2), digestImpl, true, factory(digestImpl)), prefix)
469 
470         case _ => literalsNeeded
471       }
472 
473     register("field_Based_ID", fieldBasedID(MessageDigestFactory), minimum = 3)
474     register("za_Longs_Field_Based_ID", fieldBasedID(ZALongTupleHashFunctionFactory), minimum = 3)
475     register("za_Field_Based_ID", fieldBasedID(ZALongHashFunctionFactory), minimum = 3)
476     register("hash_Field_Based_ID", fieldBasedID(HashFunctionFactory(_)), minimum = 3)

489     val prefixedToLongPair = (exps: Seq[Expression]) =>
490       exps.size match {
491         case 2 =>
492           PrefixedToLongPair(exps(1), getString(exps.head, 0))

498     val rngID = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
499       val (randomSource, seed: Long, prefix) =
500         exps.size match {
501           case 1 => ( RandomSource.XO_RO_SHI_RO_128_PP, 0L, getString(exps.head, 0))
502           case 2 => ( getRandom(exps(1), 1), 0L,  getString(exps.head, 0))
503           case 3 => ( getRandom(exps(1), 1), getLong(exps(2), 2),  getString(exps.head, 0))
504           case _ => literalsNeeded
505         }
506 
507       GenericLongBasedIDExpression(model.RandomID,
508         RandLongsWithJump(seed, randomSource), prefix)
509     }
510     register("rng_ID", rngID, Set(1,2,3))

512     val uniqueID = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
513       val (prefix) =
514         exps.size match {

516           case _ => literalsNeeded
517         }
518

520         GuaranteedUniqueID() // defaults are all fine, ms just relates to definition instead of action
521         , prefix
522       )
523     }

569     def msgAndExpr(msgDefault: String, exps: Seq[Expression]) = exps match {
570       case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType), e: Expression) =>

591     register("qualityfunn", exps => {
592       val args = exps.dropRight(4)
593       val params = exps.drop(args.length)
594       val (function, name, attemptCodeGen, useAsLambda) =
595         params match {
596           case Seq(f, n, a, u) => (f, {
597             val s = getString(n)
598             if (s.isEmpty)
599               None
600             else
601               Some(s)
602           }, getBoolean(a), getBoolean(u))
603         }
604       FunN(args, function, name, attemptCodeGen = attemptCodeGen, usedAsLambda = useAsLambda)
605     }, minimum = 5)
